granted for this discharge.

    Sr.No. Condition Type       The refinery is permitted to operate at the expanded capacity
                              without exceeding the earlier stipulated pollution load of 24 TPD of
   AIR QUALITY                SO2 emissions. SO2 emission report may be made on a daily basis
                              for all the stacks (fuel burning and process emissions) through the
1  MONITORING AND             computerized monitoring mechanism as per the format attached.
                              Further, regular monitoring of stacks every fortnight must also be
   PRESERVATION               carried out to cross check the data obtained from computerized
                              monitoring by engaging a reputed organization such as NEERI. In
                              addition, a monthly S-balance statement indicating type of crude, its
                              S-content, product S-content, SO2 emission etc. may be made. Daily,
                              fortnightly and monthly reports generated as above should be sent to
                              the GPCB, CPCB & MoEF.

  PPs Submission: Complied                                                                                 Date:
Regular monitoring and measurement are carried out for measuring total SO2 emission from the             22/11/2024
refinery complex which is below the limits prescribed. SO2 emission monitoring report is included
in Annexure I-A. The refinery now has continuous online emission monitoring system in which the
SO2 emissions are captured in real time. Each stack is manually monitored on a monthly basis to
cross check the computerised monitoring. A MoEF approved agency has been engaged for the
monitoring. Please refer Annexure 2-A. Monthly Sulphur balance statements are prepared as
stipulated. Please refer Annexure I-A.

3  MONITORING AND           consultation with SPCB, based on occurrence of maximum ground

13  Marine/Coastal    The marine water quality shall be regularly monitored for the water

                    (primary productivity, benthos, fish quality and growth, biomass,

                    shall be continued and upgraded for the expansion and modernization
                    of the refinery complex.

prescribed standards. Refer Annexure 7C. The return seawater is discharged into the Gulf through
the existing multiport diffuser at the location identified by NIO.
